    echo "[*] Harvesting recent system errors, crashes, and permission rejections..."
    if command -v logcat >/dev/null 2>&1

    end

    rish -c "cmd audio set-parameters mic_mute=0" 2>/dev/null

        android.permission.INTERNET \

        "PROJECT_MEDIA allow" \
